<span class="token function">update</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">getDown</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Fire"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token keyword">this</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>rainbowPrefab<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">instantiate</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre></div><h4 id="getplayerconfig"><a href="#getplayerconfig" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getPlayerConfig</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getPlayerConfig</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>player<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  gamepadIndex<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token keyword">undefined</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
  useMouseAndKeyboard<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Retrieves the input configuration for a specific player, given its index.</p> <h3 id="types"><a href="#types" class="header-anchor">#</a> Types</h3> <h4 id="gamepadaxes-type"><a href="#gamepadaxes-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> GamepadAxes Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">type</span> <span class="token class-name">GamepadAxes</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  x<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  y<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  mult<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>x<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> y<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
  up<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  down<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  left<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  right<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  mult<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>xMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> yMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the representation of the axes bindings for a Gamepad. These can be either a object with either or both x and y fields, or an array with a set of buttons. In both cases we can pass in a multiplier to determine the sensitivity and direction.</p> <p>In the case of the object, we can pass both or either the x and y parameters, making it effectively a one dimensional vector. Likewise, in the case of the array we can omit some directions to receive input only for a certain direction that we care about.</p> <p>The x and y parameters should be the index for an axis in the gamepad, same as with the <a href="/EngineAPI/Input/GamepadController">GamepadController</a>. These are used for the analog sticks.</p> <p>The up/down/left/right will take the button number in a gamepad, same as with the <a href="/EngineAPI/Input/GamepadController">GamepadController</a>. This is suitable, for instance, to define movement with the d-pad.</p> <p><strong>Example</strong></p> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token comment">// Trigger the Move action using the left stick</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Gamepad<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>x<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> y<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token comment">// Trigger the Move action for player 2 using the d-pad</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Gamepad<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">12</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">13</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">14</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">15</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token comment">// You can optionally pass in a multiplier vector to adjust the sensitivity and direction.</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Gamepad<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">12</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">13</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">14</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">15</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0.5</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">0.5</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token comment">// Trigger the Look function with the right stick and pass in an optional multiplier vector </span>
<span class="token comment">// to adjust the sensitivity and direction.</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Look"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Gamepad<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>x<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> y<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">3</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> mult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>x<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">5</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> y<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><h4 id="keyboardaxes-type"><a href="#keyboardaxes-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> KeyboardAxes Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">type</span> <span class="token class-name">KeyboardAxes</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
  up<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  down<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  left<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  right<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  mult<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>xMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> yMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the representation of the axes bindings for the Keyboard. These are passed in the form of an array of standard key strings and an optional multiplier to determine the sensitivity and direction.</p> <p>we can omit some directions to receive input only for a certain direction that we care about, making it effectively a one dimensional vector.</p> <p><strong>Note:</strong> see <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/KeyboardEvent/code/code_values" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">KeyboardEvent.code<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a> for a list of the accepted code values.</p> <p><strong>Example</strong></p> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token comment">// Trigger the Move action using WASD</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Keyboard<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">"KeyW"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"KeyS"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"KeyA"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"KeyD"</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><h4 id="mouseaxes-type"><a href="#mouseaxes-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> MouseAxes Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">type</span> <span class="token class-name">MouseAxes</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>xMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> yMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the representation of the mouse movement binding. It takes an array to define the sensitivity and direction on the x and y axis respectively.</p> <p><strong>Example</strong></p> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token comment">// Trigger the Look action using the Mouse movement with full sensitivity</span>
<span class="token comment">// and with the y axis flipped.</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Look"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Mouse<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><h4 id="touchaxes-type"><a href="#touchaxes-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> TouchAxes Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">type</span> <span class="token class-name">TouchAxes</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  area<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"left"</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token string">"right"</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token string">"view"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  mult<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>x<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> y<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  normalize<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>radiusX<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> radiusY<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
  up<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  down<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  left<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  right<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> 
  mult<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>xMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> yMult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the representation of the axes bindings for the <a href="/EngineAPI/Input/TouchController">TouchController</a>. These can be either a object with the touch area and a normalize option, or an array with a set of buttons. In both cases we can pass in a multiplier to determine the sensitivity and direction.</p> <p>In the case of the object, we must pass in the area to be either the entire "view", the "left" half or the "right" half. The normalize field lets us define a distance in pixels where our movement will go from 0 to 1 like an analog stick. Very useful for things like movement.</p> <p>The up/down/left/right will take the button index in <a href="/EngineAPI/Input/TouchController#buttons">RE.Input.touch.buttons</a>. This is suitable, for instance, to define movement with touch buttons on the screen, such as the steering in a car game.</p> <p><strong>Example</strong></p> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token comment">// Trigger the Move action using the left half of the touch screen. We pass in the optional normalize array</span>
<span class="token comment">// to set a maximum travel distance of 10, effectively turning the left side of the touch screen into an analog stick</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Touch<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>area<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"left"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> normalize<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">10</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">10</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token comment">// Trigger the Move action for player 2 using 4 touch buttons with their given index at RE.Input.touch.buttons</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Touch<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">3</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token comment">// You can optionally pass in a multiplier vector to adjust the sensitivity and direction.</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Move"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Touch<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">3</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0.5</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">0.5</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token comment">// Trigger the Look function with the right half of the touch screen and pass in an optional multiplier vector </span>
<span class="token comment">// to adjust the sensitivity and direction.</span>
<span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Input<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">bindAxes</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Look"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>Touch<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>area<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"right"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> mult<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><h4 id="inputaction-type"><a href="#inputaction-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> InputAction Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">type</span> <span class="token class-name">InputAction</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">[</span>name<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token operator">:</span> InputAxesBinds <span class="token operator">|</span> InputBinds<span class="token punctuation">;</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre></div><h4 id="inputaxesbinds-type"><a href="#inputaxesbinds-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> InputAxesBinds Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>type InputAxesBinds = {
  type: "Axes",
  Gamepad?: <a href="#gamepadaxes-type">GamepadAxes</a>,
  Keyboard?: <a href="#keyboardaxes-type">KeyboardAxes</a>,
  Mouse?: <a href="#mouseaxes-type">MouseAxes</a>,
  Touch?: <a href="#touchaxes-type">TouchAxes</a>,
}
</code></pre></div> <p>This is the representation of the bindings for an axes-based action. Click on each type to see their definitions.</p> <h4 id="inputbinds-type"><a href="#inputbinds-type" class="header-anchor">#</a> InputBinds Type</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">type</span> <span class="token class-name">InputBinds</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  type<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"Button"</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
  Keyboard<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
  Mouse<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token string">"WheelUp"</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token string">"WheelDown"</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
  Gamepad<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
  Touch<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span> <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token string">"Tap"</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the representation of the bindings for a button-based action.</p> <p>In the case of the Gamepad, the number represents a gamepad button same as for the <a href="/EngineAPI/Input/GamepadController">GamepadController</a>.</p> <p>In the case of the Keyboard, the string reprensents the standard <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/KeyboardEvent/code/code_values" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">KeyboardEvent.code<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a></p> <p>In the case of the Mouse, we can pass in the standard button number or "WeelUp"/"WeelDown" to capture the sequential wheel movement.</p> <p>In the case of the Touch, we can pass in the button index as defined in <a href="/EngineAPI/Input/TouchController#buttons">RE.Input.touch.buttons</a> or alternatively, the "Tap" action detects a single tap on the screen.</p></div>

<div class="theme-default-content content__default"><h3 id="mouse"><a href="#mouse" class="header-anchor">#</a> Mouse</h3> <p>The <strong>Mouse</strong> class keeps track of mouse events and provides access to these interactions. This class is instanced by the <a href="/EngineAPI/Input">Input</a> controller, and that's the only place where it should be used. For that reason, this class is not exposed by the engine.</p> <h2 id="properties"><a href="#properties" class="header-anchor">#</a> Properties</h2> <h4 id="x"><a href="#x" class="header-anchor">#</a> .x</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> x<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the position of the mouse on the x-axis of the screen. This is analogous to the <strong>clientX</strong> property of the <strong>MouseEvent</strong>.</p> <h4 id="y"><a href="#y" class="header-anchor">#</a> .y</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> y<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the position of the mouse on the y-axis of the screen. This is analogous to the <strong>clientY</strong> property of the <strong>MouseEvent</strong>.</p> <h4 id="viewx"><a href="#viewx" class="header-anchor">#</a> .viewX</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> viewX<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the position of the mouse on the x-axis relative to the canvas.</p> <h4 id="viewy"><a href="#viewy" class="header-anchor">#</a> .viewY</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> viewY<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the position of the mouse on the y-axis relative to the canvas.</p> <h4 id="movementx"><a href="#movementx" class="header-anchor">#</a> .movementX</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> movementX<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the movement of the mouse on the x-axis of the screen. This is analogous to the <strong>movementX</strong> property of the <strong>MouseEvent</strong>.</p> <h4 id="movementy"><a href="#movementy" class="header-anchor">#</a> .movementY</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> movementY<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This is the movement of the mouse on the y-axis of the screen. This is analogous to the <strong>movementY</strong> property of the <strong>MouseEvent</strong>.</p> <h4 id="wheelx"><a href="#wheelx" class="header-anchor">#</a> .wheelX</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> wheelX<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This represents the horizontal scrolling amount. This is analogous to the <strong>deltaX</strong> property of the <strong>WheelEvent</strong>.</p> <h4 id="wheely"><a href="#wheely" class="header-anchor">#</a> .wheelY</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> wheelY<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This represents the vertical scrolling amount. This is analogous to the <strong>deltaY</strong> property of the <strong>WheelEvent</strong>.</p> <h4 id="ismoving"><a href="#ismoving" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isMoving</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isMoving<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A handy flag which tells us if the mouse is moving.</p> <h4 id="isleftbuttondown"><a href="#isleftbuttondown" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isLeftButtonDown</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isLeftButtonDown<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the left mouse button has been pressed in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="isleftbuttonpressed"><a href="#isleftbuttonpressed" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isLeftButtonPressed</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isLeftButtonPressed<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the left mouse button is being pressed. If true, will be set to false when the button is released.</p> <h4 id="isleftbuttonup"><a href="#isleftbuttonup" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isLeftButtonUp</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isLeftButtonUp<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the left mouse button has been released in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="isrightbuttondown"><a href="#isrightbuttondown" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isRightButtonDown</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isRightButtonDown<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the right mouse button has been pressed in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="isrightbuttonpressed"><a href="#isrightbuttonpressed" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isRightButtonPressed</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isRightButtonPressed<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the right mouse button is being pressed. If true, will be set to false when the button is released.</p> <h4 id="isrightbuttonup"><a href="#isrightbuttonup" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isRightButtonUp</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isRightButtonUp<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the right mouse button has been released in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="ismidbuttondown"><a href="#ismidbuttondown" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isMidButtonDown</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isMidButtonDown<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the middle mouse button has been pressed in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="ismidbuttonpressed"><a href="#ismidbuttonpressed" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isMidButtonPressed</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isMidButtonPressed<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the middle mouse button is being pressed. If true, will be set to false when the button is released.</p> <h4 id="ismidbuttonup"><a href="#ismidbuttonup" class="header-anchor">#</a> .isMidButtonUp</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> isMidButtonUp<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A flag which tells us if the middle mouse button has been released in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="pointerlock"><a href="#pointerlock" class="header-anchor">#</a> .pointerLock</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> pointerLock<span class="token operator">:</span> PointerLockControls <span class="token operator">|</span> <span class="token keyword">undefined</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This property provides the instance of the Threejs <strong>PointerLockControls</strong> being used by this <strong>Mouse</strong> instance to handle locking the mouse pointer. Until the mouse is locked, this property remains undefined.</p> <h4 id="enabled"><a href="#enabled" class="header-anchor">#</a> .enabled</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>enabled<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Setting this property, disables or enables the mouse controls.</p> <h2 id="methods"><a href="#methods" class="header-anchor">#</a> Methods</h2> <h4 id="lock"><a href="#lock" class="header-anchor">#</a> .lock</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">lock</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token keyword">void</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Handy function to lock the mouse pointer using the three js <strong>PointerLockControls</strong> instance referenced in the <strong>pointerLock</strong> property.</p> <h4 id="unlock"><a href="#unlock" class="header-anchor">#</a> .unlock</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">unlock</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token keyword">void</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Unlocks the mouse pointer when locked.</p> <h4 id="getbuttondown"><a href="#getbuttondown" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getButtonDown</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getButtonDown</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>button<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the key with the given mouse button has been pressed in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="getbuttonpressed"><a href="#getbuttonpressed" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getButtonPressed</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getButtonPressed</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>button<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the key with the given mouse button is being pressed. If true, will be set to false when the button is released.</p> <h4 id="getbuttonup"><a href="#getbuttonup" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getButtonUp</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getButtonUp</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>button<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the key with the given mouse button has been released in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p></div>

<div class="theme-default-content content__default"><h3 id="keyboard"><a href="#keyboard" class="header-anchor">#</a> Keyboard</h3> <p>The <strong>Keyboard</strong> class contains the functionality to keep track of Keyboard actions. This class is instanced in the <strong>Input</strong> controller, and that's the only place where it should be used. For that reason, this class is not exposed by the engine.</p> <p><strong>Note:</strong> see <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/KeyboardEvent/code/code_values" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">KeyboardEvent.code<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a> for a list of the accepted code values.</p> <h2 id="methods"><a href="#methods" class="header-anchor">#</a> Methods</h2> <h4 id="getkeydown"><a href="#getkeydown" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getKeyDown</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getKeyDown</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>keyCode<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the key with the given code has been pressed in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p> <h4 id="getkeypressed"><a href="#getkeypressed" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getKeyPressed</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getKeyPressed</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>keyCode<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the key with the given code is being pressed. If true, will be set to false when the button is released.</p> <h4 id="getkeyup"><a href="#getkeyup" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getKeyUp</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getKeyUp</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>keyCode<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the key with the given code has been released in the current frame. If true, will be set to false in the next frame.</p></div>

<div class="theme-default-content content__default"><h3 id="touchcontroller"><a href="#touchcontroller" class="header-anchor">#</a> TouchController</h3> <p>The <strong>TouchController</strong> class keeps track of touch events and provides access to these interactions. This class is instanced by the <a href="/EngineAPI/Input">Input</a> controller, and that's the only place where it should be used. For that reason, this class is not exposed by the engine.</p> <h2 id="properties"><a href="#properties" class="header-anchor">#</a> Properties</h2> <h4 id="starttouches"><a href="#starttouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .startTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> startTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that have started in the current frame.</p> <h4 id="endtouches"><a href="#endtouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .endTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> endTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that have ended in the current frame.</p> <h4 id="touches"><a href="#touches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .touches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> touches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that are still happening in the current frame.</p> <h4 id="rightstarttouches"><a href="#rightstarttouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .rightStartTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> rightStartTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that have started in the current frame on the right half of the screen.</p> <h4 id="rightendtouches"><a href="#rightendtouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .rightEndTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> rightEndTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that have ended in the current frame on the right half of the screen.</p> <h4 id="righttouches"><a href="#righttouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .rightTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> rightTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that are still happening in the current frame on the right half of the screen.</p> <h4 id="leftstarttouches"><a href="#leftstarttouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .leftStartTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> leftStartTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that have started in the current frame on the left half of the screen.</p> <h4 id="leftendtouches"><a href="#leftendtouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .leftEndTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> leftEndTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that have ended in the current frame on the left half of the screen.</p> <h4 id="lefttouches"><a href="#lefttouches" class="header-anchor">#</a> .leftTouches</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> leftTouches<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchInteraction<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>An array of <a href="/EngineAPI/Input#touchinteraction-type">TouchInteraction</a> objects which contains all touch events that are still happening in the current frame on the left half of the screen.</p> <h4 id="enabled"><a href="#enabled" class="header-anchor">#</a> .enabled</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>enabled<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This property is used to enable and disable the <strong>TouchController</strong></p> <h4 id="buttons"><a href="#buttons" class="header-anchor">#</a> .buttons</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>buttons<span class="token operator">:</span> TouchButton<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A list of active <a href="#touchbutton-type">TouchButtons</a> which can be used as part of the action-based input system. You can create them by calling <a href="#createbutton">createButton</a>.</p> <h2 id="methods"><a href="#methods" class="header-anchor">#</a> Methods</h2> <h4 id="init"><a href="#init" class="header-anchor">#</a> .init</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">init</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token keyword">void</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method initializes the touch events for the <strong>TouchController</strong>. This is used internally by the engine and you should never need call it.</p> <h4 id="createbutton"><a href="#createbutton" class="header-anchor">#</a> .createButton</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">createButton</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>elem<span class="token operator">:</span> HTMLDivElement<span class="token punctuation">,</span> stopPropagation<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">boolean</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> TouchButton
</code></pre></div><p>Takes an HTMLDivElement and creates a <a href="#touchbutton-type">TouchButton</a> which can be used as part of the action-based input system. <div class="theme-default-content content__default"><h3 id="gamepadcontroller"><a href="#gamepadcontroller" class="header-anchor">#</a> GamepadController</h3> <p>The <strong>GamepadController</strong> class contains the functionality to keep track of Gamepad interactions. This class is instanced in the <strong>Input</strong> controller, and that's the only place where it should be used. For that reason, this class is not exposed by the engine.</p> <p>This is just an abstraction of the <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Gamepad" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Gamepad<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a> API. To learn about the mapping of all the buttons of a controller visit <a href="https://gamepad-tester.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gamepad-tester.com<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a></p> <h2 id="props"><a href="#props" class="header-anchor">#</a> Props</h2> <h4 id="deadzone"><a href="#deadzone" class="header-anchor">#</a> .deadZone</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>axesErrorMargin<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token number">0.1</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Axes most usually have a margin of error. Their rest value will not be 0. If the raw absolute value of an axis is smaller or equal to <code>axesErrorMargin</code>, <code>getAxis</code> will return <code>0</code>.</p> <h4 id="gamepad"><a href="#gamepad" class="header-anchor">#</a> .gamepad</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> gamepad<span class="token operator">:</span> Gamepad<span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This property holds the <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Gamepad" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Gamepad<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a> object associated to this <code>GamepadController</code>.</p> <h2 id="methods"><a href="#methods" class="header-anchor">#</a> Methods</h2> <h4 id="getaxis"><a href="#getaxis" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getAxis</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getAxis</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>index<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method returns the numeric value of the axis (from -1 to 1) at the given index. Most usually <code>0</code> and <code>1</code> will be the horizontal and vertical axes of the left stick respectively, while <code>2</code> and <code>3</code> will be the horizontal and vertical axes of the right stick.</p> <p>To learn about the mapping of all the buttons for the Gamepad API visit <a href="https://gamepad-tester.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gamepad-tester.com<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a></p> <h4 id="getbutton"><a href="#getbutton" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getButton</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getButton</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>index<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method returns the numeric value (from 0 to 1) for the button at the given index. a value of <code>0</code> means the button is not being pressed and <code>1</code> means it's fully pressed.</p> <p>This uses a value since, some buttons like the left and right triggers (usually <code>6</code> and <code>7</code> respectively), are listed as buttons and not axes.</p> <p>To learn about the mapping of all the buttons of a controller visit <a href="https://gamepad-tester.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gamepad-tester.com<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a></p> <h4 id="getbuttondown"><a href="#getbuttondown" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getButtonDown</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getButtonDown</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>index<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the button at the given index has been pressed in the current frame. If true, it will return false in the next frame.</p> <p>To learn about the mapping of all the buttons of a controller visit <a href="https://gamepad-tester.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gamepad-tester.com<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a></p> <h4 id="getbuttonup"><a href="#getbuttonup" class="header-anchor">#</a> .getButtonUp</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">getButtonUp</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>index<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">number</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div><p>This method tells us if the button at the given index has been released in the current frame. If true, it will return false in the next frame.</p> <p>To learn about the mapping of all the buttons of a controller visit <a href="https://gamepad-tester.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gamepad-tester.com<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a></p></div>

<div class="theme-default-content content__default"><h3 id="prefab"><a href="#prefab" class="header-anchor">#</a> Prefab</h3> <p>A Prefab is a predefined <a href="https://threejs.org/docs/#api/en/core/Object3D" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Object3D<span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="15" height="15" class="icon outbound"><path fill="currentColor" d="M18.8,85.1h56l0,0c2.2,0,4-1.8,4-4v-32h-8v28h-48v-48h28v-8h-32l0,0c-2.2,0-4,1.8-4,4v56C14.8,83.3,16.6,85.1,18.8,85.1z"></path> <polygon fill="currentColor" points="45.7,48.7 51.3,54.3 77.2,28.5 77.2,37.2 85.2,37.2 85.2,14.9 62.8,14.9 62.8,22.9 71.5,22.9"></polygon></svg> <span class="sr-only">(opens new window)</span></span></a> that we store in a <strong>.roguePrefab</strong> file along with all components associated to the object itself or its children.</p> <p>An instance of this class provides the information to load a Prefab and the means to instantiate it.</p> <p>The class includes some static members to allow the loading dynamic loading of Prefabs.</p> <h2 id="static"><a href="#static" class="header-anchor">#</a> Static</h2> <h4 id="static-namedprefabuuids"><a href="#static-namedprefabuuids" class="header-anchor">#</a> static namedPrefabUUIDs</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">static</span> namedPrefabUUIDs<span class="token operator">:</span> Record<span class="token operator">&lt;</span><span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token operator">&gt;</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>A map of all prefab <code>uuids</code> with their paths relative to <code>Assets/Prefabs/</code> as keys. We refer to this relative path as the "name path" and all Prefabs in <code>Assets/Prefabs/</code> as "Named Prefabs". A name path, does not include the <code>.roguePrefab</code> extension.</p> <h4 id="static-instantiate"><a href="#static-instantiate" class="header-anchor">#</a> static instantiate</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">static</span> <span class="token function">instantiate</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>name<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">Promise</span><span class="token operator">&lt;</span><span class="token constant">THREE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Object3D<span class="token operator">&lt;</span><span class="token constant">THREE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Object3DEventMap<span class="token operator">&gt;&gt;</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Asynchronously instatiate a prefab within <code>Assets/Prefabs/</code>, by their "name path", or relative path to the Prefabs folder, excluding the <code>.roguePrefab</code> extension. This will fetch the prefab, including all its dependencies and instantiate it in your current scene.</p> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>
<span class="token keyword">async</span> <span class="token function">start</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="token comment">// Location: Assets/Prefabs/MyPrefab.roguePrefab</span>

  <span class="token keyword">const</span> nemesis <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token keyword">await</span> <span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>prefab<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">instantiate</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Enemies/Nemesis"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>

</code></pre></div><h4 id="static-fetch"><a href="#static-fetch" class="header-anchor">#</a> static fetch</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">static</span> <span class="token function">fetch</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>name<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">Promise</span><span class="token operator">&lt;</span>Prefab<span class="token operator">&gt;</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Asynchronously fetch a prefab within <code>Assets/Prefabs/</code>, by their "name path", or relative path to the Prefabs folder, excluding the <code>.roguePrefab</code> extension.</p> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code>

  <span class="token keyword">const</span> nemesisPrefab <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token keyword">await</span> <span class="token constant">RE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>prefab<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">fetch</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Enemies/Nemesis"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>

<span class="token punctuation">}</span>

</code></pre></div><h4 id="static-get"><a href="#static-get" class="header-anchor">#</a> static get</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">static</span> <span class="token function">get</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>name<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> Prefab<span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>Synchronously fetch a prefab within <code>Assets/Prefabs/</code>, by their "name path", or relative path to the Prefabs folder, excluding the <code>.roguePrefab</code> extension. </code></pre></div><p>The unique identifier for the Prefab.</p> <h4 id="path"><a href="#path" class="header-anchor">#</a> .path</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> path<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>The current path to the Prefab file.</p> <h4 id="name"><a href="#name" class="header-anchor">#</a> .name</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token keyword">readonly</span> name<span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token builtin">string</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span>
</code></pre></div><p>The name of the Prefab.</p> <h2 id="methods"><a href="#methods" class="header-anchor">#</a> Methods</h2> <h4 id="instantiate"><a href="#instantiate" class="header-anchor">#</a> .instantiate</h4> <div class="language-typescript extra-class"><pre class="language-typescript"><code><span class="token function">instantiate</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>parent<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token constant">THREE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Object3D<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token constant">THREE</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>Object3D<span class="token punctuation">;</span>
